                                                             Chairman’s Statement

統一企業中國控股有限公司（「本公司」）及其                    Uni-President China Holdings Ltd. (the “Company”) and its
附屬公司（統稱「本集團」或「我們」）秉持「誠                   subsidiaries (together as the “Group”, “our Group”, “we”, “our” or
實勤道、創新求進」的經營理念，堅持「價值營                    “us”) adhere to the corporate vision of “Honesty and Diligence;
銷」，聚焦經營，積極構建品牌價值，走穩健經                    Innovation and Excellence（誠實勤道、創新求進）” and uphold the
營之道，以「健康操作」為指導原則，致力於成                    principle of “Value Marketing（價值營銷）”. With a focus on operation,
為一個具備「品格」、
         「品牌」與「品味」的三品企                   we are always vigorous in creating brand value, pursuing steady
業。                                       operation and aligning to “Healthy Practice（健康操作）”, with an aim
                                         to evolve into an enterprise boasting “Integrity（品格）”, “Brand（品
                                         牌）” and “Taste（品味）”.

靠 著 經 驗 豐 富 的 經 營 團 隊、穩 健 的 獲 利 模 式      With experienced management team, robust profit-making model
與風險控管機制，不斷優化組織營運結構，妥                     and risk management mechanism, the Group progressed steadily
善因應市場變動所帶來的系統性風險，2024                    in the complex and ever-changing economic environment and set
年本集團在複雜多變的經濟環境中仍保持穩                      another record for revenue and profit in 2024, as it continuously
健前行，收益與利潤再創新高，為未來實現更                     optimised its organisational and operational structure to properly
雄 偉 目 標 奠 定 了 堅 實 的 基 礎。中 國 經 濟 持 續      cope with the systemic risks brought about by market fluctuations,
復甦，消費市場呈現新特徵新趨勢，對我們來                     which formed a solid foundation for achieving greater ambitions in
說 既 是 考 驗 也 是 機 遇，如 何 為 消 費 者 提 供 更      the future. Further recovery of China’s economy and new characters
具價值的消費體驗始終是我們潛心研究的課                      and trends of the consumption market represent both challenges
題。作為與民生息息相關的食品飲料企業，憑                     and opportunities to us, and it is a major theme to which we have
藉 著 全 體 同 仁 的 不 懈 努 力、靈 敏 的 組 織 韌 性      been devoted for so long to provide consumers with more valuable
與風險管理機制，得以乘風破浪穿越週期，行                     consumption experience. As a food and beverage enterprise that
穩而致遠。自2020年以來，本集團收益年複合                   is closely related to people’s well-being, we maintained stable
增長率達7.4%，其中飲品年收益複合增長率                    development and reached further goals after braving the wind and
更是達11.3%。                                waves and sailing the growth cycle by the unremitting endeavour of
                                         all staff, the resilient organisation and the flexible risk management
                                         mechanism. Since 2020, the Group’s revenue has been growing at
                                         a compound annual growth rate of 7.4%, in which the compound
                                         annual growth rate of the beverages even reached 11.3%.

提高投資效益，全方位擴大國內需求」做為首                     key task to “vigorously boost consumption, improve investment
要 工 作 任 務，中 國 經 濟 有 望 進 一 步 增 強 內 需      efficiency, and expand domestic demand on all fronts”, and it is
驅動，並保持穩定增長。隨著政策持續支持，                     expected that China’s economy will be fueled by the strengthened
更 有 利 於 推 動 消 費 復 甦 態 勢 向 好。在 新 的 經      drive of domestic demand and sustain stable growth. The continuous
濟 格 局 下，本 集 團 將 持 續 穩 固 各 個 市 場 的 基      policy support will stimulate consumption recovery more efficiently
礎 建 設 及 組 織 能 力，強 化 營 運 系 統 建 設 及 人      to a better outlook. In the new economic landscape, the Group
力資源的培育，為消費者提供具有安全、品質                     will further consolidate infrastructure construction and organisation

               統一企業中國控股有限公司 Uni-President China Holdings Ltd.    • Annual Report 2024 年報                             7
    管理層討論及分析
    Management Discussion & Analysis

    經濟環境分析                                   ANALYSIS ON ECONOMIC ENVIRONMENT

    中華人民共和國（「中國」）2024年國內生產總                  In 2024, the gross domestic product (“GDP”) of the People’s
    值(GDP)同比增長5.0%，社會消費品零售總額                 Republic of China (the “PRC” or “China”) recorded a year-on-
    同比增 長3.5%。2024年 是 中 國 的「消 費 促 進          year increase of 5.0% and total retail sales of consumer goods
    年」，消費依然是經濟增長最重要的驅動力。                     grew by 3.5% year-on-year. The year 2024 was positioned as the
    中 國 政 府 實 施 一 系 列 擴 內 需、促 消 費 政 策，       “Consumption-Promoting Year（消費促進年）” in the PRC, and the
    通過完善「購物節、消費月、首發季、促進年」                    consumption remained the most important driving forces for the
    的消費活動體系，進一步釋放消費潛力、提振                     economic growth. The PRC government has implemented a series
    消費信心，消費市場呈現逐步復甦態勢，為本                     of policies to expand domestic demand and promote consumption,
    集團實現收益穩健增長提供了有力的市場支                      and has further unleashed the potential of consumption and boosted
    持。                                       consumer confidence by perfecting the consumer activity system
                                             of “Shopping Festival, Consumption Month, Launch Season and
                                             Promotion Year（購物節、消費月、首發季、促進年）”. The consumer
                                             market has shown a trend of gradual recovery, which has provided
                                             strong market support for the Group to achieve steady growth in
                                             revenue.

    2024年食品飲料相關原物料市場價格走勢持                    The market price trend of raw materials related to food and beverage
    續波動。地緣政治緊張局勢、匯率波動、通貨                     remained volatile in 2024. Multiple risks such as geopolitical tensions,
    膨 脹 等 多 重 風 險，給 全 球 經 濟 復 甦 與 供 應 鏈      exchange rate fluctuations and inflation have brought about various
    的 安 全 穩 定 帶 來 諸 多 不 確 定 性。本 集 團 持 續      uncertainties to the global economic recovery and the stability of the
    關 注 外 部 環 境 的 變 化，不 斷 提 升 組 織 協 調 能      supply chain. The Group continued to pay attention to the changes in

上，包 括 對 沖 基 金 或 類 似 的 金 融 產 品。本 年        leverage or risks, including hedge funds or similar financial products.
度，本 集 團 於 財 務 風 險 管 理 方 面 繼 續 維 持 審      The Group continued to adopt a conservative approach to financial
慎態度，並無重大銀行借貸。由於本集團大部                     risk management with no significant bank borrowing during the Year.
分 收 入 源 自 中 國 的 業 務，故 大 部 分 資 產 收 款      Most of the Group’s receipts and payments were denominated in
及 付 款 貨 幣 均 以 人 民 幣 計 值。本 集 團 若 因 以      Renminbi since a majority of its revenue was derived from operations

 現 有 管 理 團 隊 的 活 力 與 戰 力。為 實 現 年 度 制      performance and secured talents for key positions of the Group.
 定 的 各 項 目 標，本 集 團 設 立 績 效 獎 金 及 獎 勵      Meanwhile, priorities were given to internal promotion over external
 計 劃，嘉 許 及 鼓 勵 於 本 集 團 業 務 作 出 傑 出 貢      recruitment, and performance appraisal and other measures were
 獻 的 各 級 僱 員。績 效 獎 金 根 據 本 集 團 整 體 以      implemented to keep the current management team ambitious
 及 各 業 務 單 位 達 成 之 收 益、利 潤 等 目 標 以 及      and strong. To achieve the Group’s annual goals, the Group had
 僱員的績效評核發放。                               performance bonuses and incentive schemes in place to commend
                                          and encourage employees at all levels to make outstanding
                                          contributions to the Group’s business. Performance bonuses were
                                          distributed on the basis of the realized earnings and profits objectives
                                          of individual business units and the Group as a whole, as well as the
                                          performance appraisal of the respective employees.

 本 集 團 酬 金 政 策 依 據 僱 員 及 董 事 的 表 現、資      The Group’s remuneration policy rewarded our employees and

 表現作出回報。作為本集團酬金政策的一環，                     demonstrated capabilities, market comparable information and the
 本 集 團 與 其 僱 員 訂 立 個 別 僱 傭 合 約，當 中 涉      performance of the Group. As part of the Group’s remuneration

 Report of the Directors

 於釐定上述交易是否根據相關的持續關連交                      When determining whether the above transactions have been
 易 協 議 項 下 之 定 價 政 策 進 行 時，本 公 司 管 理      conducted in accordance with the pricing policies under the relevant
 層將取得充分市場資訊以確定相關當前市                       CCT Agreements, the management of the Company would obtain
 價、付款條款及常規，以及最近期之市場及獨                     sufficient market intelligence to ascertain the relevant prevailing
 立第三方資料。例如，管理層將審閱並以商業                     market rate, payment terms and practices, as well as the latest
 角度評估來自至少兩名獨立供應商或服務供                      market and independent third party information. For example, the
 應 商 之 報 價，以 確 保 統 一 企 業 集 團 將 採 購 之      management would review quotations from at least two independent
 產品或服務價格與同一期間由獨立第三方所                      suppliers or service providers and evaluate them from a commercial
 提 供 之 該 等 產 品 或 服 務 相 若。倘 並 無 足 夠 可      perspective, so as to ensure that the products or services to be

        本集團產品的需求下降，從而導致庫存                          preferences could result in reduced demand for the Group’s
        過剩和銷量減少。本集團將在季度內繼                          products, which would, in turn, lead to excessive inventory

        期，以改進我們的產品組合並滿足消費                          introduce new products throughout the seasons and will further

(iv)    供應鏈風險                               (iv)    Supply chain risk

        本集團生產食品使用的全部包裝材料及                           All of the packaging materials used in the manufacture of the
        生產飲料產品使用的多數包裝材料均由                           Group’s food products and a large percentage of packaging
        獨立第三方或關聯方供應商提供。若該                           materials used in the production of its beverage products
        等獨立第三方或關聯方供應商無法繼續                           are supplied by independent third parties or related party
        供應或無法滿足本集團對該等包裝材料                           suppliers. In the event these independent third parties or
        的需求，或本集團無法就持續供應達成                           related party suppliers fail to continue to supply or cannot
        合理條款的協議，則本集團可能無法尋                           meet the Group’s demand for such packaging materials, or the
        求可替代的類似包裝材料供應商，從而                           Group is unable to reach agreement upon reasonable terms
        可能導致對客戶送貨延遲。該等送貨延                           with them in relation to such continued supply, the Group
        遲可能影響本集團的營運業績。本集團                           may be unable to find a comparable substitute supplier of
        已與相關供應商維持穩定的合作關係，                           packaging materials, which in turn may lead to delay in the
        以採購必需的原材料，並與三名或三名                           delivery of its products to customers. Such delivery delay may
        以上供應商發展長期戰略夥伴關係並制                           affect the Group’s results of operations. The Group strives to
        定生產戰略，旨在降低因過分依賴單一                           maintain stable and cooperative relationships with suppliers in
        供應商而產生的風險。                                  respect of the procurement of essential raw materials and to
                                                    develop long-term strategic partnerships as well as productivity
                                                    strategies with three or more suppliers to reduce the risk on
                                                    being over dependent on a single supplier.

 情投資決定。                                   make informed investment decisions relating to the Company.

 向股東宣派中期股息或特別股息。                          Company may declare interim dividends or special dividends to the
                                          Shareholders from time to time.

 及 其 不 時 可 能 認 為 相 關 的 任 何 其 他 因 素。本      capital requirements, liquidity position and any other factors it may
 公司派付任何股息亦須遵守開曼群島的適用                      deem relevant from time to time. Any payment of the dividend by
 法律及組織章程。                                 the Company is also subject to the applicable laws of the Cayman
                                          Islands and the Articles of Association.

董事會多元化政策                                 BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY

董事會已採納董事會多元化政策（於2022年5                   The Board adopted a Board Diversity Policy (as amended on 27 May
月27日修訂），旨在提升董事會效率及維持企                    2022) which aims to enhance the effectiveness of the Board and
業 管 治 之 最 高 水 平，以 及 認 同 並 確 信 董 事 會      to maintain the highest standards of corporate governance and
成員多元化之裨益。                                recognises and embraces the benefits of having a diverse Board
                                         composition.

根 據 董 事 會 多 元 化 政 策，為 了 達 到 在 董 事 會      Under the Board Diversity Policy, in order to achieve a diversity of
內有多元化的觀點、技能及經驗，在決定委任                     perspectives, skills and experience within the Board, a number of
任何人選加入董事會及繼續該等委任時將考                      factors will be considered when deciding on appointments to the
慮多項因素，包括但不限於性別、年齡、文化                     Board and the continuation of those appointments, including but
及教育背景、服務年期、技能、專業經驗、地                     not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational background,
區 及 行 業 經 驗。在 形 成 董 事 會 多 元 化 的 觀 點      length of service, skills, professional experience, regional and industry
時，本 公 司 亦 將 計 及 其 本 身 的 業 務 模 式 及 不      experience. In forming its perspectives on Board diversity, the
時的特定需要。                                  Company will also take into account its own business model and
                                         specific needs from time to time.

董 事 會 認 為，性 別 多 元 對 實 現 董 事 會 成 員 多      The Board considers that gender diversity is vital in order to achieve
元化至關重要。為達致董事會性別多元化，董                     a diverse Board composition. To achieve gender diversity within the
事會的目標為在組成上至少有1名女董事。在                     Board, the Board’s objective is to have at least 1 female director in
蘇崇銘先生於2024年5月31日退任後，在8名                  its composition. Subsequent to the retirement of Mr. Su Tsung-Ming

考 慮 合 資 格 的 女 性 候 選 人。通 過 在 董 事 會 繼      to ensuring that qualified female candidates are given equal
任 規 劃 過 程 中 強 調 性 別 多 元 化，本 公 司 旨 在      consideration in the nomination of Directors. By focusing on gender
於董事會出現空缺時維持多元化的候選人渠                      diversity in the Board succession planning process, the Company
道。                                       aims to maintain a diverse pipeline of candidates for Board vacancies

風險管理及內部監控                                RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL
                                         CONTROL

董事會整體負責維持本集團穩健而有效之風                      The Board has overall responsibilities for maintaining a sound and
險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 系 統。該 系 統 包 含 一 個 清      effective risk management and internal control system of the Group.
晰 明 確 界 定 權 限 之 管 理 架 構，旨 在 協 助 本 集      The system includes a defined management structure with limits of
團 識 別 及 管 理 重 大 風 險，以 達 成 本 集 團 的 整      authority, and is designed to help the Group to identify and manage
體業務目標，釐定本集團的風險狀況，監督風                     significant risks to achieve the Group’s overall business objectives,
險管理框架結構，審核主要風險及緩解戰略，                     determine the Group’s risk profile, oversee risk management
以及保障風險管理的成效及遵守相關法律法                      framework structure, review key risks and mitigation strategies and to
規。                                       ensure risk management effectiveness and compliance with relevant
                                         laws and regulations.

本 集 團 利 用 自 下 而 上 及 自 上 而 下 的 方 式，確      A bottom-up and top-down approach is utilised to ensure a holistic
保 擁 有 一 套 完 善 的 風 險 管 理 程 序。自 下 而 上      risk management process. The bottom-up approach is supported by
的方式以核心業務單位識別風險及確定風險                      key business units to identify and prioritise risks while the top-down
評級為基礎；而自上而下的方式則審閱及評                      approach reviews and assesses if risks are comprehensively
估核心業務單位於實現本集團的目標時是否                      identified, prioritised and properly addressed by key business units
已 全 面 識 別 風 險、為 風 險 劃 分 級 別 及 予 以 妥      to accomplish the Group’s objectives. The management team
善處理。管理層團隊定期監測新出現的風險，                     regularly monitors emerging risks for continuous risk assessment
以 進 行 持 續 風 險 評 估，建 立 以 風 險 管 理 為 基      purpose and for building the risk-management based internal audit
礎 的 內 部 審 核 計 劃，並 週 期 性 評 估 風 險 管 理      plan and periodically assesses the adequacy and effectiveness of
及 內 部 控 制 的 適 當 性 及 有 效 性，以 保 證 持 續      risk management and internal controls for ongoing risk assurance
的 風 險 擔 保。此 舉 提 高 本 集 團 風 險 管 理 及 控      purposes. This enhances the effectiveness of the Group’s risk
制框架的有效性。                                 management and control framework.




               統一企業中國控股有限公司 Uni-President China Holdings Ltd.   • Annual Report 2024 年報                       81
 企業管治報告
 Corporate Governance Report

 本集團的風險管理及內部監控系統旨在降低                      The Group’s risk management and internal control system is
 風險、保障本集團資產、防止及偵查欺詐行為                     designed to reduce risks, safeguard the Group’s assets, prevent
 及 保 障 股 東 投 資，亦 確 保 妥 善 保 存 會 計 記 錄      and detect fraud and protect shareholders’ investment as well
 以 及 遵 守 適 用 的 法 規 及 規 例。於 本 年 度，董        as to ensure that proper accounting records are maintained and
 事 會 透 過 與 管 理 層、內 部 審 計 團 隊 及 外 聘 核      applicable legislation and regulations are complied with. During the
 數 師 進 行 討 論 的 方 式，以 及 內 部 審 計 團 隊 所      Year, through discussions with the management, the internal audit
 呈 交 報 告，從 不 同 角 度 評 估 與 審 閱 本 集 團 風      team and external auditors and with reference to a report submitted
 險管理及內部監控系統之有效性，包括（其中                     by the internal audit team, the Board has conducted assessments
 包括）財務監控、營運與合規監控以及風險管                     and reviews of the effectiveness of the Group’s risk management
 理。風 險 管 理 及 內 部 控 制 系 統 每 年 由 董 事 會      and internal control system in various perspectives, including, among
 審核且每次審核範圍覆蓋整個財政年度。                       others, financial control, operational and compliance controls and
                                          risk management. The risk management and internal control systems
                                          are reviewed by the Board annually and each review covers a whole
                                          financial year.

 本 公 司 內 部 審 計 單 位 隸 屬 於 董 事 會，在 董 事      The internal audit unit of the Company, which is subordinated to the
 會 之 授 權 範 圍 內 及 審 核 委 員 會 指 導 下，與 外      Board, formulates the internal audit plan of the Group based on the

 意見的基礎                                    BASIS FOR OPINION

 我 們 已 根 據 香 港 會 計 師 公 會 頒 佈 的《香 港 審      We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards
 計準則 》 （「香港審計準則」      ）進行審計。我們在           on Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities

 表及我們的核數師報告。                              included in the annual report other than the consolidated financial
                                          statements and our auditor’s report thereon.

 我們對綜合財務報表的意見並不涵蓋其他信                      Our opinion on the consolidated financial statements does not cover
 息，我 們 亦 不 對 該 等 其 他 信 息 發 表 任 何 形 式      the other information and we do not express any form of assurance
 的鑒證結論。                                   conclusion thereon.

 結 合 我 們 對 綜 合 財 務 報 表 的 審 計，我 們 的 責      In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial statements,
 任是閱讀其他信息，在此過程中，考慮其他信                     our responsibility is to read the other information and, in doing so,
 息是否與綜合財務報表或我們在審計過程中                      consider whether the other information is materially inconsistent with
 所了解的情況存在重大牴觸或者似乎存在重                      the consolidated financial statements or our knowledge obtained in

責任。合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證                     for the contents of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high level
按照《香港審計準則 》進行的審計，在某一重                    of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in
大 錯 誤 陳 述 存 在 時 總 能 發 現。錯 誤 陳 述 可 以      accordance with HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement
由 欺 詐 或 錯 誤 引 起，如 果 合 理 預 期 它 們 單 獨      when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are
或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依                      considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could

的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。                             taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.

     集團不能持續經營。                                Group to cease to continue as a going concern.

 •   評價綜合財務報表的整體列報方式、結                   •    Evaluate the overall presentation, structure and content of the
     構 和 內 容，包 括 披 露，以 及 綜 合 財 務 報            consolidated financial statements, including the disclosures,
     表是否中肯反映相關交易和事項。                          and whether the consolidated financial statements represent
                                              the underlying transactions and events in a manner that
                                              achieves fair presentation.

 •   計劃及進行集團審計，就貴集團內實體                   •    Plan and perform the group audit to obtain sufficient
     或業務單位的財務信息獲取充足、適當                        appropriate audit evidence regarding the financial information
     的審計憑證，以便對綜合財務報表發表                        of the entities or business units within the Group as a basis for
     意 見 提 供 基 礎。我 們 負 責 指 導、監 督 及            forming an opinion on the consolidated financial statements.
     檢討就集團審計而進行的審計工作。我                        We are responsible for the direction, supervision and review of
     們為審計意見承擔全部責任。                            the audit work performed for purposes of the group audit. We

2   重大及其他會計政策概要                            2    SUMMARY OF MATERIAL AND OTHER
                                                ACCOUNTING POLICIES

          附屬公司指本集團具有控制權之                              A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity)
          實體（包括結構實體）      。本集團就參                      over which the Group has control. The Group controls an
          與該實體之可變回報承受風險或                              entity where the Group is exposed to, or has rights to,
          擁 有 權 利，並 有 能 力 通 過 對 該 實                   variable returns from its involvement with the entity and
          體 之 權 力 影 響 該 等 回 報 時，即 本                   has the ability to affect those returns through its power
          集 團 控 制 對 該 實 體。附 屬 公 司 由                   over the entity. Subsidiaries are consolidated from the
          控制權轉移至本集團當日開始綜                              date on which control is transferred to the Group. They
          合 入 賬，並 由 失 去 控 制 權 當 日 開                   are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.
          始不再綜合入賬。

          集 團 內 公 司 間 之 交 易、結 餘 及 交                   Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised
          易 之 未 變 現 利 得 均 予 以 對 銷。未                   gains on transactions between group companies are
          變 現 虧 損 亦 予 以 對 銷。附 屬 公 司                   eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated. When
          所 匯 報 金 額 已 按 需 要 作 出 調 整，                  necessary, amounts reported by subsidiaries have
          以確保與本集團之會計政策保持                              been adjusted to conform with the Group’s accounting
          一致。                                         policies.

          (a)   業務合併                                 (a)   Business combinations

          本集團應用會計收購法將業務合                              The Group applies the acquisition method of accounting
          併 入 賬。就 收 購 一 間 附 屬 公 司 轉                   to account for business combinations. The consideration
          讓 之 代 價 為 所 轉 讓 資 產、所 產 生                   transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary is the
          負債及本集團所發行股權的公平                              fair values of the assets transferred, the liabilities
          值。所 轉 讓 代 價 包 括 或 然 代 價 安                   incurred and the equity interests issued by the Group.
          排所產生任何資產或負債的公平                              The consideration transferred includes the fair value
          值。於 業 務 合 併 中 收 購 的 可 識 別                   of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent
          資產、承擔的負債及或然負債，初                             consideration arrangement. Identifiable assets acquired
          步 按 收 購 日 期 的 公 平 值 計 量。本                   and liabilities and contingent liabilities assumed in a
          集團以公平值或非控股權益應佔                              business combination are measured initially at their fair
          被收購方可識別淨資產的已確認                              values at the acquisition date. The Group recognises
          金 額 的 比 例，按 逐 項 收 購 基 準 確                   any non-controlling interest in the acquiree on an
          認任何於被收購方的非控股權益。                             acquisition-by-acquisition basis, either at fair value or at
                                                      the non-controlling interest’s proportionate share of the
                                                      recognised amounts of acquiree’s identifiable net assets.

          收購相關成本於產生時支銷。                               Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.

          資產而言的初步賬面值。此外，先                             joint venture or financial asset. In addition, any amounts
          前於其他全面收益內確認與該實                              previously recognised in other comprehensive income in
          體有關的任何金額按猶如本集團                              respect of that entity are accounted for as if the Group
          直接處置相關資產或負債之方式                              had directly disposed of the related assets or liabilities.
          入 賬。此 舉 可 能 意 味 著 先 前 於 其                   This may mean that amounts previously recognised in

          類至損益表。                                      statement.

          2.2.2 獨立財務報表                               2.2.2 Separate financial statements

          於附屬公司的投資以成本扣除減                              Investments in subsidiaries are accounted for at cost less
          值 列 賬。成 本 亦 包 括 投 資 的 直 接                   impairment. Cost also includes direct attributable costs of
          應 佔 費 用。本 公 司 按 應 收 股 息 基                   investment. The results of subsidiaries are accounted for
          準將附屬公司的業績列賬。                                by the Company on the basis of dividend receivable.

          倘從附屬公司的投資所收取的股                              Impairment testing of the investments in subsidiaries is
          息超出該附屬公司於股息宣派期                              required upon receiving dividends from these investments
          間 的 全 面 收 益 總 額，或 倘 該 投 資                   if the dividend exceeds the total comprehensive income
          於獨立財務報表的賬面值超出投                              of the subsidiary in the period the dividend is declared or
          資對象淨資產（包括商譽）於綜合                             if the carrying amount of the investment in the separate
          財 務 報 表 的 賬 面 值，則 須 於 收 取                   financial statements exceeds the carrying amount in the

          行減值測試。                                      assets including goodwill.

    2.8 非金融資產減值                             2.8 Impairment of non-financial assets

      無確定可使用年期之無形資產毋                              Intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life are
      須攤銷，而會每年進行減值測試，                             not subject to amortisation and are tested annually for
      或當有事件出現或事態變動顯示                              impairment, or more frequently if events or changes in
      可能出現減值時進行更頻密減值                              circumstances indicate that they might be impaired.
      測 試。其 他 資 產 當 事 件 出 現 或 事                   Other assets are tested for impairment whenever events
      態變動顯示未必能收回賬面值時                              or changes in circumstances indicate that the carrying
      進 行 減 值 測 試。減 值 虧 損 按 資 產                   amount may not be recoverable. An impairment loss is
      賬面值超過其可收回金額之差額                              recognised for the amount by which the asset’s carrying
      確 認。可 收 回 金 額 為 資 產 公 平 值                   amount exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable
      減出售成本及使用價值兩者之較                              amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs of
      高者。就評估減值而言，資產乃於                             disposal and value in use. For the purposes of assessing
      按具有獨立可識別現金流入的最                              impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest levels
      低 水 準，很 大 程 度 上 獨 立 於 其 他                   for which there are separately identifiable cash inflows
      資產或資產組別之現金流入（現金                             which are largely independent of the cash inflows from
      產生單位）分組。出現減值之非金                             other assets or groups of assets (cash-generating units).
      融資產（商譽除外）則於各報告期                             Non-financial assets other than goodwill that suffered
      末就其減值之撥回可能性進行檢                              an impairment are reviewed for possible reversal of the
      討。                                          impairment at the end of each reporting period.

      計量類別：                                       measurement categories:

           他 全 面 收 益 或 計 入 損 益）之                        (either through OCI or through profit or loss), and
           金融資產，及

      •    按攤銷成本計量之金融資                            •     those to be measured at amortised cost.
           產。

          設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部                              Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are
          分或全部融資將會很有可能提                               recognised as transaction costs of the loan to the extent
          取，該 費 用 可 遞 延 確 認 為 交 易 成                   that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be

      2.19 撥備                                   2.19 Provisions

          倘本集團需就過去事項承擔現有                              Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present
          法 律 或 推 定 責 任，而 有 可 能 導 致                   legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events;
          資 源 流 出 以 履 行 該 責 任，並 能 可                   it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required
          靠 估 計 金 額 才 會 確 認 撥 備。並 無                   to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably
          就未來經營虧損確認撥備。                                estimated. Provisions are not recognised for future
                                                      operating losses.

          倘 出 現 多 項 類 似 債 務，會 否 導 致                   Where there are a number of similar obligations, the
          經濟利益流出以清償債務乃經考                              likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement is
          慮 債 務 之 整 體 類 別 後 確 定。即 使                   determined by considering the class of obligations as a
          同類別債務中任何一項可能流出                              whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of
          經 濟 利 益 之 機 會 不 大，仍 會 確 認                   an outflow with respect to any one item included in the
          撥備。                                         same class of obligations may be small.

          撥備乃按管理層對清償債項所需                              Provisions are measured at the present value of
          開 支 之 最 佳 估 計 之 現 值，使 用 反                   management’s best estimate of the expenditure required
          映市場目前所評估之金錢時間價                              to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects
          值，以 及 該 項 負 債 特 定 之 風 險 之                   current market assessments of the time value of money
          除 稅 前 稅 率 計 算。因 時 間 的 流 逝                   and the risks specific to the liability. The increase in the
          導致的撥備增加則確認為利息開                              provision due to the passage of time is recognised as
          支。                                          interest expense.

    2.24 或然負債及或然資產                          2.24 Contingent liabilities and contingent assets

       或然負債乃一項因過往事件可能                             A contingent liability is a possible obligation that arises
       產 生 的 承 擔，而 該 等 過 往 事 件 的                  from past events and whose existence will only be
       存在僅可由一項或多項並非由本                             confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one
       集團全權控制的日後不明朗事件                             or more uncertain future events not wholly within the
       的 存 在 與 否 確 定。或 然 負 債 亦 可                  control of the Group. It can also be a present obligation
       為一項因不大可能需要耗用經濟                             arising from past events that is not recognised because
       資源或承擔之金額未能可靠地計                             it is not probable that outflow of economic resources
       算而未確認之過往事件產生之現                             will be required or the amount of obligation cannot be
       有承擔。                                       measured reliably.

       或 然 負 債 未 予 以 確 認，惟 已 於 財                  A contingent liability is not recognised but is disclosed
       務 報 表 中 披 露。倘 耗 用 經 濟 資 源                  in the financial statements. When a change in the
       之可能性出現變動致使有可能需                             probability of an outflow occurs so that outflow is
       耗 用 經 濟 資 源，則 或 然 負 債 將 確                  probable, it will then be recognised as a provision.
       認為撥備。

       或然資產指因為已發生的事件而                             A contingent asset is a possible asset that arises from
       可 能 產 生 的 資 產，此 等 資 產 只 能                  past events and whose existence will be confirmed only
       根據本集團不能完全控制的一項                             by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more
       或多項不確定事件的出現與否方                             uncertain events not wholly within the control of the
       能確認。                                       Group.

       或 然 資 產 未 予 確 認，惟 於 可 能 出                  A contingent asset is not recognised but is disclosed in
       現經濟利益流入時於財務報表附                             the notes to the financial statements when an inflow of
       註 中 披 露。若 經 濟 利 益 流 入 可 被                  economic benefits is probable. When inflow is virtually
       實 質 確 定 時，則 對 該 資 產 進 行 確                  certain, an asset is recognised.
       認。

          入 賬。不 符 合 對 沖 會 計 準 則 之 衍                   value of these derivative instruments that do not qualify

          有法律上可強制執行權利抵銷已                              amount reported in the balance sheet when there is a

          或 同 時 變 現 資 產 及 清 償 負 債 時，                  and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or realise
          在 資 產 負 債 表 列 報 之 淨 額。法 律                   the asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The

          後事件而定及必須為正常業務中                              events and must be enforceable in the normal course

      2.26.4 持作出售的非流動資產                         2.26.4 Non-current assets held for sale

      值計量的金融資產及投資性物業                             assets and investment property that are carried at fair
      等訂明可獲豁免遵守該規定的資                             value and contractual rights under insurance contracts,
      產則除外。                                      which are specifically exempt from this requirement.

      減值虧損乃於資產初始或其後撇                             An impairment loss is recognised for any initial or
      減至公平值扣除出售成本時確                              subsequent write-down of the asset to fair value less
      認。當 資 產 公 平 值 減 出 售 成 本 其                  costs to sell. A gain is recognised for any subsequent
      後有任何增加時，即確認利得，惟                            increases in fair value less costs to sell of an asset,
      金額不得超過先前確認的任何累                             but not in excess of any cumulative impairment loss
      計 減 值 虧 損。於 非 流 動 資 產 的 出                  previously recognised. A gain or loss not previously
      售日期前並未確認的利得或虧損                             recognised by the date of the sale of the non-current
      於終止確認日期確認。                                 asset is recognised at the date of derecognition.

      非流動資產在分類為持作出售時                             Non-current assets are not depreciated or amortised

  3   財務風險管理                               3    FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT

      3.1 財務風險因素                                3.1 Financial risk factors

          本集團的活動使本集團面臨不同                              The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial
          財 務 風 險 ： 市 場 風 險（包 括 外 匯                   risks: market risk (including foreign exchange risk and
          風 險、現 金 流 量 和 公 平 值 利 率 風                   cash flow and fair value interest rate risk), credit risk
          險）、信 貸 風 險 及 流 動 資 金 風 險。                   and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management
          本集團的整體風險管理計劃集中                              program focuses on the unpredictability of financial
          於 金 融 市 場 之 不 可 預 見 性，並 力                   markets and seeks to minimize potential adverse effects
          求最大限度地降低對本集團之財                              on the Group’s financial performance.
          務表現之潛在不利影響。

          (a)   市場風險                                 (a)   Market risk

          (i)   外匯風險                                 (i)   Foreign exchange risk

          本 集 團 實 行 國 際 化 經 營，並 承 受                   The Group operates internationally and is exposed to
          各種貨幣風險所產生的外匯風                               foreign exchange risk arising from various currency
          險，主 要 涉 及 功 能 貨 幣 為 人 民 幣                   exposures, primarily with respect to the USD for certain
          的 若 干 中 國 附 屬 公 司 之 美 元。外                   PRC subsidiaries whose functional currency are RMB.
          匯 風 險 來 自 未 來 商 業 交 易、已 確                   Foreign exchange risk arises from future commercial
          認的資產及負債以及外國業務的                              transactions, recognised assets and liabilities and net
          投資淨額。                                       investments in foreign operations.

          管 理 層 已 制 定 政 策，要 求 集 團 公                   Management has set up a policy to require Group
          司 管 理 其 功 能 貨 幣 的 外 匯 風 險。                  companies to manage their foreign exchange risk
          為管理未來商業交易及已確認資                              against their functional currency. To manage their foreign
          產 及 負 債 產 生 的 外 匯 風 險，本 集                   exchange risk arising from future commercial transactions
          團實體在中國以外地方使用遠期                              and recognised assets and liabilities, entities in the Group
          合約，並由本集團財務處置。當未                             use forward contracts outside the PRC, transacted by

        蓋如下文(iv)所述延期選擇權。                                               extension options as described in (iv) below.

        各 種 不 同 條 款 及 條 件。除 出 租 人                                      contain a wide range of different terms and conditions.

  27 其他儲備（續）                                27 OTHER RESERVES (CONTINUED)

      (b)   法定儲備                                 (b)   Statutory reserves

            中國法律與法規要求在中國註冊                             The PRC laws and regulations require companies
            的 公 司，在 對 權 益 持 有 人 作 溢 利                  registered in the PRC to provide for certain statutory
            分派前從各自的法定財務報表所                             reserves, which are appropriated from the net profit as
            申報的溢利淨額（在抵銷以往年度                            reported in their respective statutory financial statements
            的累計虧損後）中提撥一定的法定                            after offsetting accumulated losses from prior years and
            儲 備。所 有 法 定 儲 備 乃 就 特 定 目                  before profit distributions to equity holders. All statutory
            的而增設。                                      reserves are created for specific purposes.

            作為外商獨資企業和內資企業註                             PRC subsidiaries incorporated as wholly-foreign owned
            冊 成 立 的 中 國 附 屬 公 司，在 進 行                  enterprises and domestic companies are required to
            當 年 度 除 稅 後 溢 利 分 派 時，須 將                  appropriate 10% of statutory net profits to statutory
            法 定 溢 利 淨 額 之10%撥 作 法 定 盈                  surplus reserves, upon distribution of their post-tax
            餘 公 積 金，直 至 法 定 盈 餘 公 積 金                  profits of the current year, until the statutory surplus
            不少於公司之註冊資本50%為止。                           reserve is not less than 50% of its registered capital. In
            此 外，此 等 公 司 可 在 各 自 董 事 會                  addition, at the discretion of the respective boards of
            酌情決定下將部分除稅後溢利撥                             directors, these companies may allocate a portion of their
            入職工福利及花紅儲備及任意盈                             post-tax profits to the staff welfare and bonus reserve
            餘 公 積 金。作 為 中 外 合 資 經 營 企                  and discretionary surplus reserve. PRC subsidiaries
            業 註 冊 成 立 的 中 國 附 屬 公 司，在                  incorporated as sino-foreign equity joint venture
            其各自董事會酌情決定下將部分                             companies may allocate a portion of their statutory post-
            法定除稅後溢利撥入法定儲備。                             tax profits to the statutory reserves at the discretion of
                                                       the respective boards of directors.

            法定盈餘公積金只能用於彌補公                             The statutory surplus reserves shall only be used to make
            司 的 虧 損、擴 大 公 司 的 生 產 經 營                  up losses of the companies, to expand the companies’
            或增加公司資本。                                   production operations, or to increase the capital of the
                                                       companies.
